These results demonstrate that combining temporal sequence learning with vertically informed attention mechanisms provides a physically meaningful pathway for improving forecast error prediction in operational NWP systems. Our research offers forecasters enhanced guidance regarding model bias and forecast confidence.

</details>


### [144] [Anomaly Detection for Sparse and Irregular Multivariate Time Series with Latent SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18898)
*Martin Uray,Dominik Geng,Florian Graf,Stefan Huber,Roland Kwitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜在随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）的生成式方法，用于解决多变量时间序列异常检测中数据稀疏、不规则采样和缺失观测等实际问题，在六个基准数据集上取得了最优结果，并在数据稀疏条件下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSAD方法假设时间序列均匀采样，而真实数据往往稀疏、不规则采样或部分缺失，缺乏能够自然处理这些现实不规则性的异常检测方法。

Method: 提出基于潜在随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）的生成式方法，将观测时间序列投影到连续时间随机动力系统上，直接处理缺失观测和不规则采样，同时自然捕获真实数据中可能存在的循环行为。

Result: 在六个异常检测基准数据集上，所提方法在所有基线方法中排名第一；在严重数据稀疏条件下仍保持鲁棒性，而基线方法性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 潜在随机微分方程可作为多变量时间序列异常检测的自然归纳偏置，特别是在存在现实不规则性（稀疏、不规则采样、缺失数据）的场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) is critical for a wide range of application areas, such as industrial monitoring, cybersecurity, or healthcare. Real-world data is often sparse, irregularly sampled or partially observed, yet existing methods assume uniformly sampled time series. We propose a generative approach based on Latent SDEs that projects the observed time series on a continuous-time stochastic dynamical system, directly being able to handle missing observations and irregular sampling, while also naturally capturing possible cyclic behavior that many real-world use cases inherently possess. Experiments on six anomaly benchmark datasets show that our proposed method ranks first among state-of-the-art baselines. We further demonstrate that our method remains robust under severe data sparsity, while performance significantly degrades for the tested baseline methods. These results highlight latent SDEs as a natural inductive bias for anomaly detection in multivariate time series, especially in presence of real-world irregularities.

</details>


### [145] [Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19134)
*Arnaud Lequen,Clément Legrand-Lixon,Léo Saulières*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PQLRM结合了Pareto Q-Learning和Reward Machines，提出了一种多目标强化学习算法，可在非马尔可夫奖励结构下高效学习Pareto最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理非马尔可夫奖励结构的多目标强化学习任务时，要么样本效率低（如朴素PQL），要么无法生成Pareto最优策略（如QRM），因此需要一种兼具两者优势的算法。

Method: 将Pareto Q-Learning（维护向量值Q估计的Pareto前沿）与Q-Learning with Reward Machines（利用奖励信号的因子化自动机结构）相结合，形成多策略学习算法。

Result: 实验表明，PQLRM收敛速度快于基于交叉乘积MDP的朴素PQL基线，并且能够合成QRM无法获得的Pareto最优策略。

Conclusion: PQLRM在RM编码的非马尔可夫奖励任务中，实现了样本高效的Pareto最优多策略学习，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We present Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines (PQLRM), a multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm for tasks whose reward structure is specified by a set of reward machines (RMs). PQLRM combines Pareto Q-Learning (PQL), which maintains sets of vector-valued Q-estimates to approximate the Pareto front, with enhancements from Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM), which exploits the factored automaton structure of the reward signal. This yields a multi-policy algorithm that remains sample-efficient under non-Markovian, RM-encoded rewards. Experimental trials show that PQLRM converges faster than a naive PQL baseline applied to the cross-product MDP and can synthesize Pareto-optimal policies that QRM cannot.

</details>


### [146] [OrthoReg: Orthogonal Regularization for Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19145)
*Till Richter,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合建模中神经网络可能重新学习已发现的符号结构导致冗余和不解释的问题，本文提出正交正则化（OrthoReg），直接惩罚符号和神经组件之间的重叠，实现互补分解，提升符号恢复和分布外行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合建模中，神经组件可能重新学习已由符号组件捕获的机械结构，导致冗余和不可解释的模型，特别是在符号结构本身从数据中发现时；现有基于L2正则化的方法在符号组件通过稀疏发现学习时失效。

Method: 提出OrthoReg（正交正则化），直接惩罚符号组件与神经组件之间的重叠，迫使二者互补：符号部分捕获库能表达的结构，神经部分捕获剩余部分。

Result: 在部分库不匹配的基准动力系统上，OrthoReg改善了符号恢复性能和分布外泛化行为。

Conclusion: OrthoReg有效防止符号结构被神经残差吸收，实现了符号与神经组分的互补分解，提升了混合建模的可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamical systems are fundamental to modeling the natural world, yet modeling them involves a persistent trade-off: manually prescribed mechanistic models are interpretable by design but often overly simplistic and misspecified; in contrast, flexible data-driven neural methods lack physical insight. Hybrid modeling aims for the best of both worlds by combining a prescribed or symbolic, physics-based component with a flexible neural network. A critical challenge, however, is that the neural component may relearn mechanistic parts, yielding redundant and uninterpretable models, especially when the symbolic structure itself is discovered from data. Existing methods based on standard $L^2$ regularization rely on a projection argument that breaks when the symbolic component is learned through sparse discovery, allowing the neural augmentation to overlap with symbolic structure. We introduce \textbf{OrthoReg} (Orthogonal Regularization), which directly penalizes overlap between the symbolic and neural components, preventing symbolic structure from being absorbed by the neural residual. This yields a complementary decomposition: the symbolic part captures what the library can express, and the neural part captures what remains. On benchmark dynamical systems with partial library mismatch, OrthoReg improves symbolic recovery and out-of-distribution behavior.

</details>


### [147] [Some Complexity Results for Robustness Verification for Binarized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18918)
*Harshit Goyal,Sudakshina Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了二值化神经网络（BNN）的可满足性和鲁棒性验证问题的计算复杂度，证明可满足性是NP完全的，而均匀遮挡下的鲁棒性可在多项式时间内检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究BNN验证问题的计算复杂度边界，理解哪些验证问题是可处理的，哪些是计算困难的。

Method: 通过从布尔可满足性问题（SAT）归约证明BNN可满足性为NP完全；通过分析均匀遮挡下网络输出的分段常数结构，设计多项式时间鲁棒性检查算法。

Result: BNN可满足性问题是NP完全的；均匀遮挡鲁棒性可在多项式时间内判定。

Conclusion: BNN的验证问题存在复杂度二分：一般可满足性难解，但特定扰动模式（均匀遮挡）下的鲁棒性可以高效求解。

Abstract: This paper studies the computational complexity of verification problems for Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), where activations (and sometimes weights) are binary. We analyze two problems: satisfiability and robustness under uniform image occlusion. We show that BNN satisfiability is NP-complete via a reduction from Boolean satisfiability problem (SAT), and that uniform occlusion induces a piecewise-constant structure in the network output, enabling a polynomial-time robustness-checking algorithm.

</details>


### [148] [Essential Subspace Merging for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19164)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Xin Geng,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于本质子空间分解(ESD)的模型合并方法，通过将任务更新分解为主成分方向来减少任务间干扰，并在此基础上提出无训练静态合并方法ESM和动态合并方法ESM++，实验证明能有效保留任务知识并减少干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并面临的核心挑战是任务特定参数更新之间的跨任务干扰，现有方法难以有效解耦不同任务的知识。

Method: 提出本质子空间分解(ESD)，将任务更新按其激活偏移的主成分进行分解；基于ESD提出无训练静态合并方法ESM（正交化并融合本质组件）；进一步扩展为ESM++，将任务特定残差分解为低秩专家并通过原型路由选择最相关专家。

Result: 在多个任务集和模型规模上的实验表明，ESM和ESM++能有效保留任务知识并减少跨任务干扰。

Conclusion: 通过对任务更新进行本质子空间分解，可以有效分离任务知识并减少合并时的干扰，静态和动态方法均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Model merging aims to enable multi-task learning by integrating the capabilities of multiple models fine-tuned from the same pre-trained checkpoint into a single model. Its core challenge is inter-task interference among task-specific parameter updates. In this paper, we analyze the output shifts induced by task updates and observe that their energy is concentrated in a small number of principal directions. We call the subspace spanned by these directions the essential subspace. In contrast, most remaining directions carry little task-relevant energy, but their accumulation across multiple task updates can cause severe interference during merging. Motivated by this observation, we propose Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD), which decomposes each task update according to the principal components of its activation shift. Based on ESD, we introduce Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static merging method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into one compact multi-task model. We further extend ESM to ESM++, a training-free dynamic merging method that decomposes task-specific residuals into low-rank experts and selects the most relevant expert through prototype-based routing during forward inference. Extensive experiments across multiple task sets and model scales demonstrate that ESM and ESM++ effectively preserves task knowledge while reducing inter-task interference.

</details>


### [149] [GrapNet: A Programmable Dynamic-Architecture Neural Graph Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18923)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GrapNet提出一种以图即网络的可编程神经网络框架，将计算图视为可编辑的一等接口，支持关系删除、子图冻结、局部函数审计和后端切换等结构化操作，在Split Fashion-MNIST和Split CIFAR-10上分别比基线提升12.08和3.81个精度点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定张量神经网络缺乏一等可编程接口，编辑关系、冻结子图、审计局部函数或更换执行后端都需要ad-hoc参数手术，而非对神经程序本身的操作。

Method: GrapNet将图视为架构与可执行程序，每个计算节点拥有下一层子引用和可训练分配向量；删除关系即物理移除子引用及对应坐标。结构规则和执行策略位于节点核心之外，支持生长、冻结、结构编辑、分组成可训练族块、注意力路由及拓扑稳定后降维为稠密快照。通过向量值父接口与稠密层、CNN编码器、ResNet、注意力块和Transformer表示兼容。

Result: 在Split Fashion-MNIST上，可塑GrapNet+ER头达63.16% seen-class准确率，比参数量更大的稠密MLP+ER高12.08个点(p=1.3e-5)；在Split CIFAR-10上，使用冻结ImageNet ResNet-18编码器，比MLP-256在线头高3.81个点(p=0.0026)。

Conclusion: GrapNet是一种可编辑的神经图基板，其核心价值在于结构可编程性与忠实的执行视图。

Abstract: Programmability is a missing first-class interface in fixed-tensor neural networks: editing a relation, freezing a subgraph, auditing a local function, or changing the execution backend should be an operation on the neural program rather than ad-hoc parameter surgery. GrapNet studies this graph-as-network setting. The graph is the architecture and executable program, not an input data graph. Each compute node owns its next-layer child references and a trainable allocation vector aligned with those references; deleting a relation physically removes both the child reference and the corresponding allocation coordinate. Structural rules and execution policies live outside the node core, so the same child-owned graph can be grown, frozen, structurally edited, grouped into trainable family blocks, routed by attention over active relations, or lowered to dense snapshots after topology stabilizes. GrapNet composes with conventional modules through a vector-valued parent interface: dense layers, CNN encoders, ResNet feature extractors, attention blocks, and transformer representations can all feed one sensory GrapNode per coordinate. The evaluation is organized as a programmability stress suite rather than as a new replay benchmark. In a matched ten-seed Split Fashion-MNIST study, a plastic GrapNet+ER head reaches 63.16 percent seen-class accuracy versus 51.08 percent for a parameter-larger dense MLP+ER under the same seen-class loss and replay memory, with paired delta 12.08 points and p=1.3e-5. On Split CIFAR-10 with a frozen ImageNet ResNet-18 encoder, the same substrate improves the online head over MLP-256 by 3.81 points, with p=0.0026. These results support GrapNet as an editable neural graph substrate whose core value is structural programmability with faithful execution views.

</details>


### [150] [Zero-Shot Active Feature Acquisition via LLM-Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18933)
*Binyamin Perets,Natalie Mendelson,Shiran Vainberg,Yehuda Chowers,Shai Shen-Orr,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本主动特征获取（AFA）框架，通过严格分离大型语言模型（LLM）的知识能力与决策能力，仅让LLM提供马尔可夫随机场（MRF）的充分统计量（单变量偏差和成对协变），解决了传统AFA依赖大量标注数据的局限性。在IBD患者队列的二元分类和top-k识别任务中，该框架优于LLM自身及现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AFA方法依赖大量标注数据来拟合概率模型，成本高昂。LLM虽具备无监督领域知识，但作为顺序规划器表现不佳，将“知识”与“决策”两种能力混杂于同一模型存在根本性缺陷。因此需要一种分离机制，在零样本条件下实现高效的AFA。

Method: 通过严谨的知识提取（elicitation），仅让LLM返回MRF的充分统计量：单变量偏差（unary deviations）和成对协变（pairwise co-variations）。针对LLM仅返回判别性统计量（区分类别而非孤立描述）导致的规范模糊性（gauge ambiguity），采用最大熵闭包（maximum-entropy closure）进行消解。将框架应用于二元分类和top-k识别两个场景。

Result: 在IBD患者队列上，该框架在真实标签和LLM自身提取的信念上都优于LLM基线。在最困难的病人子集上，top-k获取策略显著优于所有现有方法。

Conclusion: 分离知识提取与顺序决策是可行的零样本AFA范式。通过严谨的统计建模（MRF）和最大熵闭包，既能利用LLM的领域知识，又能规避其规划能力的不足，在医疗等标注稀缺领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) sequentially selects which features to observe to reach a classification or ranking decision. Its central limitation is reliance on large amount of labeled data to fit probabilistic models guiding acquisition. Large language models (LLMs) supply unsupervised domain knowledge, but are poor sequential planners. Asking one to both know and decide conflates capabilities best kept separate. Here, we develop a framework for zero-shot AFA through disciplined elicitation: asking the LLM only for what it can be trusted to return, the unary deviations and pairwise co-variations that are the sufficient statistics of a Markov random field (MRF). We apply our framework to two settings: binary classification and top-$k$ identification. In practice, the LLM reliably returns only discriminative statistics, what distinguishes the classes rather than each class in isolation, which precludes classical AFA. We apply a maximum-entropy closure that resolves this gauge ambiguity. We evaluate on a cohort of Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD) patients, an active clinical setting where diagnostic ambiguity and patient heterogeneity obstruct stable treatment strategies. Our framework outperforms the LLM both on real labels and on its own extracted beliefs. Where it matters most, on the hardest patients, our top-$k$ acquisition policy markedly outperforms all existing methods.

</details>


### [151] [Be Your Own Teacher: Steering Protein Language Models via Unsupervised Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18961)
*Lanqing Li,Shentong Mo,Yang Yu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督奖励优化框架，通过结合模型不确定性和语义一致性的任务无关奖励，利用SRO和BRO两种离线算法对蛋白质语言模型进行后训练，在无需湿实验或偏好数据集的情况下实现了可控制的蛋白质生成，显著优于DPO、KTO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型在可控生物分子设计中表现出潜力，但后训练适应通常依赖昂贵的湿实验验证或精心整理的偏好数据集，存在监督瓶颈。为克服这一局限，本文旨在探索无监督奖励优化方法。

Method: 提出无监督奖励优化框架，核心思想是构建结合内在模型不确定性与外在语义一致性的任务无关奖励（由蛋白质表示模型提供信息），并设计两种离线算法——软奖励优化（SRO）和二值化奖励优化（BRO），以最大化经典RLHF目标。

Result: 在组合式分布外提示上的实验表明，SRO和BRO显著优于DPO、KTO等竞争基线，在不同采样温度、模型规模和蛋白质家族上接近oracle性能；无监督奖励微调的PLMs在pass@k评估中覆盖率持续高于基础模型。

Conclusion: 本文框架通过利用模型自身生成的经验实现PLMs的自我改进，为在标记偏好或实验反馈稀缺的环境中实现可控制生物分子设计提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for controllable biomolecular design, yet their post-training adaptation typically relies on costly wet-lab validation or curated preference datasets. To overcome this supervision bottleneck, we introduce unsupervised reward optimization of PLMs, a comprehensive framework for steerable protein generation without ground-truth labels. Our key insight is that task-agnostic rewards, which combine intrinsic model uncertainty with extrinsic semantic consistency informed by protein representation models, exhibit strong correlation with controllability measures across base models and temperature regimes. Building upon this discovery, we propose two offline algorithms: Soft Reward Optimization (SRO) and Binarized Reward Optimization (BRO), which effectively maximize the classical RLHF objective induced by these proxy rewards. Extensive experiments on compositional out-of-distribution prompts demonstrate that both methods significantly outperform competitive baselines (DPO, KTO), while approaching oracle performance across multiple sampling temperatures, model scales and protein families. Moreover, PLMs fine-tuned with unsupervised rewards can achieve consistently higher coverage compared to their base model in pass@k evaluations. By enabling self-improvement of PLMs through their own generated experience, our framework provides a scalable pathway toward controllable biomolecular design in settings where labeled preferences or experimental feedback are scarce or unavailable.

</details>


### [152] [Forecasting what Matters: Decision-Focused RL for Controlled EV Charging with Unknown Departure Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19199)
*Giuseppe Gabriele,Fabio Pavirani,Seyed Soroush Karimi Madahi,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对电动汽车充电中关键特征（如离开时间）不可用的问题，提出一种决策聚焦的强化学习（DF-RL）框架，将预测器与充电策略进行端到端联合训练，使预测器优化目标与下游决策质量对齐，相比传统RL方法，总奖励提升14%，未供给能量减少55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车大规模接入给电力系统带来峰值需求和电网不稳定性挑战，强化学习可用于智能充电控制，但实际场景中关键特征（如离开时间）常不可用，导致RL策略学习困难；现有预测模型仅优化预测精度，未考虑预测误差对下游决策的负面影响。

Method: 提出决策聚焦强化学习（DF-RL）框架，将预测器与RL充电策略进行端到端联合训练，使预测器在训练过程中接收充电策略动作的反馈信号，从而优化预测目标以提升下游决策质量，而非仅追求预测精度。

Result: 所提DF-RL方法相较于不使用离开时间预测的RL基线方法，总奖励提升14%，未供给能量（因EV已离开而未能完成的充电量）减少55%。

Conclusion: 预测器与控制器（RL智能体）的端到端联合训练能够有效缓解关键特征不确定性对充电策略的负面影响，产生更高质量的充电决策，为实际应用中特征不可用场景下RL的部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.

</details>


### [153] [Online Reward-Punishment Learning from Fixed-Channel Perceptual Event Streams without Environment Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18963)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OHIRL框架，在无标量奖励或评价标签的在线奖惩学习环境中，仅通过固定通道的感知包和状态转移后果推断价值，分离四个模块实现奖励-惩罚信号的自主习得，并在多项任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习依赖标量奖励或评价标签，而生物体在缺乏外部奖励信号时仍能学习奖惩关联。本文旨在探索一种无需显式奖励或评估标签的在线奖惩学习框架，使智能体能仅从感知过渡后果中推断价值信号。

Method: 提出OHIRL框架，包含四个模块：M_psi（下一个感知包预测）、D_omega（残差动力学建模）、C_eta（固定内部后过渡轨迹评估器，基于恢复正向/持久增长负向的残差调节方向）、B_xi（利用价值证据进行策略更新和动作评分）。通过条件误差分解分离证据估计误差和残差策略优化误差。

Result: 在2x2-XOR任务中，药物和辣椒在不同视觉XOR上下文中获得相反价值；B_xi达到0.952平衡奖励符号准确率。在线审计中M_psi保持0.907 R²，B_xi达0.940符号准确率，策略达0.979最优动作准确率。各类对照组（即时包评分、预测误差奖励、乱序目标、零奖励、误差减少控制）均崩溃。

Conclusion: OHIRL能够在无外部奖励信号的情况下，仅通过感知过渡后果成功学习奖惩关联，且四个模块均不可或缺。系数来源审计表明等单元、原始相等和随机单调变体保留了92%以上的顶层动作排序，而符号反转保留0%，验证了C_eta设计的关键性。

Abstract: We study online reward-punishment learning when the environment provides no scalar reward or evaluative label. At each step the agent receives only a fixed-channel perceptual packet, and quantities such as pain, energy, contact, damage, or cognitive error are treated as perceptual dimensions whose valence must be inferred from transition consequences. OHIRL separates four roles: M_psi learns next-packet prediction, D_omega models residual dynamics, C_eta is a fixed internal post-transition trajectory evaluator, and B_xi learns to use the resulting value evidence for later policy updates and action scoring. C_eta uses a recovery-positive and persistence/growth-negative residual-regulation orientation; a coefficient-origin audit shows that equal-unit, raw-equal, and random monotone variants preserve more than 92% of the released top-action rankings, while sign inversion preserves 0%.
  The reward-free protocol exposes observation transitions while withholding environment rewards, delayed external evaluators, success labels, and action-goodness labels. A conditional error decomposition separates B_xi evidence-estimation error from residual policy-optimization error. In a 2x2-XOR packet task, medicine and chili acquire opposite value under visual XOR contexts, and the same pain or spice increase can be positive or negative depending on consequence structure; B_xi reaches 0.952 balanced reward-sign accuracy. In a full online-interleaved audit, M_psi reaches holdout R2=0.907, B_xi reaches 0.940 sign accuracy, and the policy reaches 0.979 optimal-action accuracy, while immediate packet scores, prediction-error rewards, shuffled targets, zero reward, and error-reduction controls collapse. Hidden-reward CartPole and Taxi controls, public-context no-leakage audits, and module-role ablations further test information boundaries and component necessity.

</details>


### [154] [Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19220)
*Diana Magalhães,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了XGBoost-Forget，一种针对XGBoost模型的机器学习遗忘方法，用于处理表格形式的网络入侵检测数据。在IoT-23和GeNIS两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持预测性能的同时显著提升了遗忘效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习遗忘研究主要集中在深度学习和图像数据领域，而网络入侵检测领域严重依赖表格数据，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出XGBoost-Forget遗忘方法，针对XGBoost模型设计，用于处理表格形式的网络入侵检测数据。

Result: 在IoT-23和GeNIS两个网络入侵表格数据集上，XGBoost-Forget在保持接近原始模型的预测性能的同时，实现了显著更快的遗忘速度。

Conclusion: XGBoost-Forget在表格形式的网络入侵检测场景中具有应用潜力，为MU技术在非深度学习领域（尤其是表格数据）的拓展提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.

</details>


### [155] [EfficientRollout: System-Aware Self-Speculative Decoding for RL Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18967)
*Minseo Kim,Minjae Lee,Seunghyuk Oh,Kevin Galim,Donghoon Kim,Coleman Hooper,Harman Singh,Amir Gholami,Hyung Il Koo,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EfficientRollout是一个面向强化学习(RL)训练阶段LLM推理加速的系统感知型自推测解码框架，通过量化自推测解码器、系统感知的推测开关策略和接受率自适应的草稿长度调整，在不影响最终模型质量的前提下，将rollout延迟降低19.6%，端到端延迟降低12.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练阶段中，LLM的rollout生成因自回归解码的顺序特性和长尾生成样本的存在而成为延迟瓶颈。虽然推测解码(SD)在固定LLM服务中能有效降低延迟，但直接应用于RL rollouts面临两大挑战：(1) 策略持续演化，任何固定草稿模型与目标策略的分布匹配度会不断下降；(2) rollout解码过程中活跃batch size逐渐缩小，使解码从计算密集型转向内存密集型，导致并行验证无法充分利用闲置算力。

Method: EfficientRollout包含三个核心组件：(1) 量化自推测解码器——从目标模型自身通过量化生成草稿模型，无需额外的草稿模型预训练或在线适配，确保草稿模型与演化中的策略保持耦合；(2) 系统感知的推测开关策略——仅在有利的计算条件下启用推测解码，避免进入计算密集型瓶颈区间；(3) 接受率自适应的草稿长度调整——根据草稿模型质量动态调整草稿预算，匹配实时的接受率变化。

Result: 在加速的自回归(AR)rollout基线上，EfficientRollout将rollout延迟降低最多19.6%，端到端延迟降低最多12.7%，同时保持最终模型质量不受影响。

Conclusion: EfficientRollout通过系统感知的自推测解码框架有效解决了RL rollout中策略演化和计算瓶颈带来的推理加速难题，在不牺牲模型质量的前提下显著降低了训练延迟。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a representative post-training paradigm for LLMs, enabling strong reasoning and agentic capabilities. However, rollout generation remains a dominant latency bottleneck because autoregressive sampling decodes responses sequentially and a small number of long-tailed generations often determine completion time. Speculative decoding (SD) offers a natural way to address this bottleneck, as it is a well-established technique for serving fixed LLMs that reduces latency by rapidly drafting tokens and accepting them through parallel verification while preserving the target-model distribution. However, its practical speedups do not directly carry over to RL rollouts: (i) the evolving target policy makes any fixed drafter increasingly mismatched with the policy's output distribution; and (ii) active batch sizes shrink throughout rollout decoding, shifting decoding from compute-bound to memory-bound regimes where parallel verification can exploit underutilized compute. Therefore, accelerating RL rollouts requires both a drafter that remains effective under long, high-temperature generations from an evolving policy and system-aware use of SD that avoids compute-bound regimes. We present EfficientRollout, a system-aware self-SD framework designed to address this gap for RL rollouts. EfficientRollout induces a quantized drafter from the target model (i.e. self-speculative decoding), keeping it coupled to the evolving policy without separate drafter pretraining or online adaptation. It further coordinates a system-aware SD toggle policy with acceptance-aware draft-length adaptation, enabling speculation only in beneficial regimes while matching the drafting budget to evolving drafter quality. EfficientRollout reduces rollout and end-to-end latency by up to 19.6% and 12.7%, respectively, over an accelerated AR rollout baseline, while preserving final model quality.

</details>


### [156] [Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19222)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAST是一种机制对齐的选择性目标遗忘方法，通过仅更新注意力投影张量的顶层子集来消除RLVR引入的推理能力，同时显著降低对保留知识的附带损害。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B和Qwen3-1.7B-Base上，MAST实现统计显著的target遗忘（MATH forget从45/150降至37/150），同时保持GSM8K（+0.8 pp）和MATH retain（-0.5 pp）性能，优于破坏保留能力的全参数更新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全参数梯度上升方法在遗忘RLVR引入的推理能力时，会严重损害模型保留的MATH和GSM8K性能。需要一种机制引导的选择性更新方法，在实现目标遗忘的同时最小化对保留能力的附带损害。

Method: MAST首先分析SFT到RLVR的增量与SFT更新的token级delta-log-probability差异，然后按离主能量（off-principal energy）、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合幅度对注意力投影张量进行排序，仅更新排名最高的子集，而非全参数更新。

Result: 在主模型上，MAST实现统计显著的目标遗忘（MATH forget从45/150降至37/150，McNemar p=0.0078），同时保留GSM8K（+0.8 pp）和MATH retain（-0.5 pp）。该优势在不同随机种子、NPO/SimNPO目标以及Qwen3模型上均得到复现，其中全参数更新导致GSM8K崩溃而MAST保持其性能。

Conclusion: MAST通过机制对齐的参数选择性更新，在保持保留知识的前提下有效消除RLVR引入的推理能力，为模型遗忘任务提供了一种低附带损害的有效方法。

Abstract: We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.

</details>


### [157] [DIPHINE: Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18997)
*Simon Pedro Galeano Munoz,Mustapha Bounoua,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi,Maurizio Filippone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIPHINE，首个基于扩散模型的神经估计器，用于连续非高斯动态系统的集成信息分解（ΦID），通过单个摊销网络联合估计所有互信息项，并通过Möbius反演恢复十六个信息原子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ΦID方法仅适用于高斯或离散系统，无法处理连续非高斯动态系统，限制了其在真实复杂系统中的应用。

Method: 利用基于分数的扩散模型构建神经估计器，从单个摊销网络中联合估计所有所需互信息项，再通过Möbius反演恢复十六个信息原子。

Result: 在合成基准上准确恢复真实原子，优于现有互信息估计器；在真实数据上提取了可生理学解释的信息动力学结构，无需任何分布假设。

Conclusion: DIPHINE有效突破了ΦID对高斯/离散系统的限制，且推断出协同-协同原子在Möbius反演中是最难估计的。

Abstract: Uncovering the true informational architecture of real-world complex systems requires disentangling how their components uniquely store, redundantly share, and synergistically integrate information over time. Integrated Information Decomposition ($Φ$ID) is a framework for decomposing the information dynamics of multivariate systems into sixteen non-overlapping atoms that characterize redundant, unique, and synergistic modes of information storage, transfer, and integration. Existing methods to compute $Φ$ID are restricted to Gaussian or discrete systems, preventing its application to continuous non-Gaussian dynamical systems. We address this limitation by proposing DIPHINE (Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator), the first neural estimator that leverages score-based diffusion models to jointly estimate all the mutual information terms required by $Φ$ID from a single amortized network, recovering the sixteen atoms through Möbius inversion. We provide a theoretical analysis of error propagation through the inversion, showing that the Jacobian of the mapping from mutual informations to atoms is integer-valued and that the synergy-to-synergy atom is provably the hardest to estimate. We demonstrate accurate recovery of ground-truth atoms on synthetic benchmarks, superior performance compared to established mutual information estimators, and the ability to extract physiologically interpretable information-dynamic structure on an application involving real data without any distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [158] [Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19317)
*Amiri Hayes,Belinda Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用可执行程序近似替代Transformer注意力头行为的方法：先收集注意力矩阵，再让预训练语言模型生成Python程序来复现注意力模式，最后重排序筛选最佳程序。实验表明，不到1000个程序即可达到75%以上的IoU相似度，替换25%的注意力头仅导致16%的困惑度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释深度学习长期追求用人类可理解的符号描述替代不透明的神经网络计算，但缺乏将神经组件（如注意力头）行为精确转化为可执行符号程序的可扩展方法。

Method: 1) 对每个注意力头，在随机训练样本上计算注意力矩阵；2) 将矩阵摘要输入预训练语言模型，生成一组Python程序来复现注意力模式；3) 根据在保留输入上的预测表现对程序进行重排序，选择最优程序集。

Result: 在GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B上，少于1000个生成程序即可达到75%以上的平均IoU相似度；替换25%的注意力头仅使困惑度平均增加16%，且在下游QA基准上保持性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可扩展的反向工程流水线，将Transformer注意力头翻译为人类可读、可执行的代码，推动了神经模型向符号透明性的进展。

Abstract: A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.

</details>


### [159] [UBP2: Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning for Efficient Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19328)
*Mohamed Nabail,Leo Cheng,Jingmin Wang,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型的方法UBP2，通过联合推理奖励、动态和值函数的不确定性来主动引导探索，在偏好强化学习中实现更高的样本效率，并提供了亚线性遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好强化学习方法通常依赖被动数据收集，在学习的早期阶段样本效率低下，缺乏主动探索策略。

Method: 提出Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2)，使用奖励、动态和值函数模型的集成来评估候选轨迹，根据结合期望奖励、终端值和认知不确定性的统一评分进行规划，实现探索与利用的显式权衡。

Result: 在标准正则性假设下，建立了有限时域和无限时域设置下的亚线性遗憾保证；在Meta-World基准上的实验表明，UBP2的样本效率显著高于无模型偏好方法和非乐观的基于模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合不确定性推理的主动探索策略可以有效提升偏好强化学习的样本效率，且具有理论保证。

Abstract: Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Geometric and Stochastic Analysis of Discontinuities in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19036)
*Tho Tran Huu,Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Thien-Hai Nguyen,Nhat-Tri Ho,Viet-Hoang Tran,Tho Quan,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对稀疏混合专家（SMoE）架构中的不连续性进行了严格的几何与随机分析，按阶次对不连续面分类，建立了体积渐近估计，证明随机路径几乎必然首次击中一阶不连续点，并据此提出一种轻量级平滑机制，在语言和视觉任务中既保证了连续性又提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMoE架构中Top-k专家选择机制导致映射函数本质上不连续，在不连续面附近，微小输入扰动可能激活完全不同的专家集合，产生显著不同的输出，这种不连续性缺乏严格的理论分析，且可能影响模型鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Method: 1) 按切换事件中并列专家数量对不连续面进行阶次分类；2) 利用测度论切片论证建立增厚不连续面的渐近体积估计；3) 将输入扰动建模为扩散过程，证明路径几乎必然遭遇不连续点，且首次击中几乎必然发生在一阶不连续点，给出有限时间概率界；4) 推导各阶不连续邻域的占据时间界；5) 提出一种平滑机制，软性地在近不连续处纳入专家，保证额外计算开销小。

Result: 低阶不连续集占主导地位，高阶不连续集相对体积趋近于零；随机路径首次击中不连续点几乎必然是一阶不连续点；提出的平滑机制在语言和视觉任务中不仅恢复了映射连续性，还提升了实际性能。

Conclusion: 输入更可能位于低阶不连续面附近，基于此洞察设计的平滑机制能有效缓解SMoE的不连续性问题，且额外计算开销可控，经验上性能有提升。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) architectures are now widely deployed in state-of-the-art language and vision models, where conditional routing allows scaling to very large networks. However, this very Top-$k$ expert selection that enables conditional routing also renders the SMoE map inherently discontinuous. In the vicinity of these discontinuity surfaces, even inputs that are arbitrarily close may activate substantially different sets of experts resulting in significantly different outputs. In this work we give a rigorous geometric and stochastic analysis of these discontinuities. We first classify them by order, determined by the number of tied experts at a switching event. Using measure-theoretic slicing arguments, we establish asymptotic volume estimates for the thickened discontinuity surfaces, showing that lower-order discontinuity sets dominate, whereas higher-order ones occupy a vanishingly small relative volume. Next, modeling random perturbations in the input space via a diffusion process, we prove that the path eventually encounter a discontinuity, and moreover that the first hit almost surely occurs on an order-1 discontinuity with explicit finite-time probability bounds. We further derive occupation-time bounds that quantify the duration the random path spend in the neighborhoods of each discontinuity order. These theoretical results imply that inputs are more likely to lie near lower order discontinuities. Motivated by this insight, we propose a simple smoothing mechanism that can be directly applied to existing SMoEs, softly incorporating experts near discontinuities; our analysis guarantees that the added computational overhead remains small while providing localized smoothing near discontinuities, and experiments across language and vision tasks show that smoothing not only enforces continuity of the SMoE map but also enhances empirical performance.

</details>


### [161] [Smoothness-Based Derandomization of PAC-Bayes Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19105)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑损失函数下的PAC-Bayes去随机化方法，通过利用损失函数和预测器类的光滑性，推导出确定性预测器的高概率泛化界。核心贡献是将从Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的转换代价量化为Jensen gap类的泛化差距，并通过Rademacher复杂度控制该类。得到的界涉及参数雅可比矩阵和得分映射海森矩阵的平坦度度量。方法适用于有界和无界光滑损失，在线性预测器和光滑神经网络上给出了特化结果。理论中出现的雅可比和海森量激发了实用的正则化器，针对BatchNorm网络，通过将BatchNorm变换折叠到相邻仿射权重中计算该正则化器。CIFAR-10实验展示了该正则化器在不同批大小下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAC-Bayes理论主要针对随机预测器（Gibbs预测器）给出泛化界，但实际应用中更常用确定性预测器。作者希望利用损失函数和预测器类的光滑性，将随机预测器的泛化保证转化为确定性预测器的高概率泛化界，并从中获得实用的正则化方法。

Method: 1) 将Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的转换代价形式化为Jensen gap类的泛化差距；2) 通过Rademacher复杂度控制Jensen gap类；3) 推导出涉及参数雅可比矩阵和得分映射海森矩阵的平坦度度量泛化界；4) 针对BatchNorm网络，通过将BatchNorm变换折叠到相邻仿射权重中计算正则化器；5) 在CIFAR-10上进行实验验证。

Result: 得到了确定性预测器的高概率泛化界，界中包含了通过参数雅可比和海森矩阵表达的平坦度项；提出了针对BatchNorm网络的实用正则化器；CIFAR-10实验展示了该正则化器在不同批大小下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了光滑损失下PAC-Bayes去随机化的理论框架，证明了从Gibbs预测器到确定性预测器转换代价的精确刻画，并由此导出了实用的正则化方法，为理解深度网络泛化与平坦度之间的关系提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study PAC-Bayes derandomization for smooth loss functions. Our goal is to obtain generalization bounds that hold with high probability for deterministic predictors by exploiting smoothness properties of both the loss and the predictor class. We show that passing from the Gibbs predictor to the deterministic predictor at the posterior mean has a precise cost, given by the generalization gap of the Jensen gap class. We control this class through its Rademacher complexity, leading to bounds for deterministic predictors that involve flatness quantities expressed in terms of parameter Jacobians and Hessians of the score map. The framework applies to both bounded and unbounded smooth loss functions, and we specialize the results to linear predictors and smooth neural networks. Finally, the Jacobian and Hessian quantities appearing in the theory motivate a practical regularizer. For BatchNorm networks, we compute this regularizer with respect to effective BatchNorm weights obtained by folding the BatchNorm transformation into the adjacent affine weights. Experiments on CIFAR-10 illustrate the behavior of this regularizer under different batch sizes.

</details>


### [162] [JourneyFormer: Encoding Airbnb Guest Journey with Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19108)
*Daochen Zha,Chun How Tan,Xin Liu,Bin Xu,Han Zhao,Xiaowei Liu,Tracy Yu,Hui Gao,Huiji Gao,Liwei He,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Airbnb提出的序列建模解决方案JourneyFormer，用于搜索排序中的用户行为序列建模，解决了长序列、稀疏标签等生产环境挑战，并通过精心设计的数据和模型策略在线上线下均取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管序列建模在推荐系统中有广泛应用，但在实际生产部署中面临序列长度大、用户行为复杂、标签稀疏（如预订标签）等挑战，需要平衡效果与可扩展性。Airbnb希望解决这些生产难题，将序列建模成功落地到搜索排序场景。

Method: JourneyFormer从多个角度进行设计优化：包括用户事件选择（如何从长序列中筛选有效行为）、ID嵌入设计、模型架构选择、标签归因策略，以及针对训练和推理加速的定制化方案。

Result: JourneyFormer已在Airbnb两个生产表面成功部署，离线排序指标提升，在线A/B测试中关键业务指标也获得显著增长。

Conclusion: JourneyFormer证明了经过精心设计的序列建模方案能够在复杂生产环境中有效部署，为工业界处理长序列、稀疏标签等实际问题提供了可借鉴的方案。

Abstract: Sequence modeling has become increasingly popular in recommendation and ranking algorithms, owing to its capacity to model users' historical behaviors and infer user intentions. Despite its theoretical simplicity, the practical deployment of a sequence model in production is non-trivial due to complexity of the sequence and sparse labels. For example, in Airbnb, guest sequences are often long, exploratory and complex, and we focus on booking labels, which are sparse. As such, we are often required to make various design decisions regarding data and modeling to strike a balance between effectiveness and scalability. This work delved into these production challenges and deployed JourneyFormer, a sequence modeling solution for search ranking at Airbnb. We detail crucial design considerations, covering aspects such as guest event selection, ID embeddings, model architecture, and label attribution. Additionally, we describe several tailored strategies to accelerate model training and inference. JourneyFormer has been successfully deployed within Airbnb's production, where its effectiveness and impact have been evidenced not only by improved offline ranking metrics but also by significant gains in key business metrics through online A/B testing across 2 production surfaces.

</details>


### [163] [Seeing Before Reasoning: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Shortcut-Resilient Multimodal On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19120)
*Sihan Wang,Xiyao Liu,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViGOS是一种针对多模态大模型的视觉接地在线自蒸馏框架，通过让学生模型先生成视觉描述再推理，并利用图像仅感知教师和特权推理教师分别监督描述和推理过程，避免直接OPSD扩展到多模态时的文本捷径问题，在多个基准测试上保持OPSD优势并改善图像接地行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将在线自蒸馏(OPSD)扩展到多模态大语言模型(MLLM)时存在捷径问题：特权目标可能主要基于文本参考目标而非图像来引导token生成，导致模型忽视视觉信息。

Method: ViGOS框架包含三个步骤：(1) 学生先写视觉描述，再进行推理得出最终答案；(2) 对有效rollout，图像仅感知教师监督描述部分，特权推理教师监督推理和最终答案（使用相同学生前缀）；(3) 对无效rollout，使用参考教师恢复输出格式。

Result: 在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间接地和视觉语言先验等多个基准测试中，ViGOS保持了OPSD的主要优势，并在易出现捷径的场景中显著改善了图像接地行为。

Conclusion: 通过引入视觉描述中间步骤和分工明确的教师监督机制，ViGOS有效解决了OPSD扩展到多模态时的文本捷径问题，使模型真正基于视觉信息进行推理。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a model on its own rollouts and uses a frozen copy to provide dense token-level targets conditioned on a reference target. This works well for LLM reasoning, but a direct extension to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can create a shortcut: the privileged target may guide tokens mainly based on the text reference target rather than the image. We propose ViGOS, a visually grounded OPSD framework for MLLM post-training. The student first writes a visual description and then reasons toward the final answer. For valid rollouts, an image-only perception teacher supervises the description, while a privileged reasoning teacher supervises the reasoning and final answer on the same student prefix. A reference teacher is used only for invalid rollouts to recover the output format. Across general vision-language, expert reasoning, visual math, spatial grounding, and visual-language-prior benchmarks, ViGOS keeps the main benefits of OPSD and improves image-grounded behavior in shortcut-prone settings.

</details>


### [164] [INDEQS: Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19138)
*Michael Detzel,Gabriel Nobis,Kristiyan Blagov,Juri Schubert,Jackie Ma,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INDEQS是一种将已知有向图先验知识融入图基神经常微分方程（NCDE）的时序预测方法，通过在架构的不同位置（内部混合与外部混合）注入图结构信息，在合成和真实世界的预测任务中显著优于未注入先验的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图基NCDE方法通常完全从数据中学习空间结构，但在许多实际场景中，有向图结构是事先已知的。作者希望探索如何有效地将已知图结构先验融入NCDE框架，并系统研究图信息在什么情况下对预测最有益。

Method: INDEQS将隐藏状态在图节点间的内部混合与向量场和控制信号之间的外部混合相分离，在架构的不同位置注入已知有向图先验。提供两种变体：轻量级的图约束变体（严格遵循已知邻接矩阵）和更具表达力的变体（通过自适应图卷积从数据中学习额外连接）。此外，对比了离散卷积解码器和连续时间解码器。

Result: 在合成数据集（有向图上的连续平流模拟）和两个真实世界任务（水文网络河流水位预测、PeMS08交通流量预测）上，外部信息注入在参数量相当的情况下一致优于未注入信息的NCDE，尤其在较大图上提升显著；内部信息注入在需要严格遵守已知邻接时提供了更参数高效的替代方案；连续时间解码器在准确性和时间灵活性上优于离散卷积解码器。

Conclusion: 在NCDE架构中注入已知图结构先验能有效提升时序预测性能，且注入位置（内/外）和方式（严格约束/自适应学习）应根据任务需求选择。连续时间解码器更适合实际预测任务。

Abstract: Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDE) provide a powerful continuous-time framework for forecasting time series, but standard graph-based extensions typically learn spatial structure purely from data, even in settings where a directed graph structure is known a priori. We introduce Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS (INDEQS), a graph-based NCDE forecasting method that incorporates prior knowledge of a directed graph at distinct architectural positions. INDEQS separates inner mixing of hidden states across graph nodes from outer mixing between vector field and control, and offers both a lightweight graph-constrained variant and a more expressive variant, learning additional graph connections from data via adaptive graph convolutions. To systematically study when graph informedness is beneficial in forecasting, we devise a continuous advection simulation on directed graphs, yielding synthetic spatio-temporal datasets with known ground-truth flow structure. We then evaluate INDEQS on two real-world tasks: river discharge forecasting on a hydrological network and traffic flow prediction on PeMS08. Across these synthetic and real-world benchmarks, outer informedness consistently improves mean absolute error over an uninformed NCDE with comparable parameter count, particularly on larger graphs, while inner informedness offers a more parameter-efficient alternative when strict adherence to a known adjacency is desired. A comparison of discrete convolutional and continuous-time decoders further shows that continuous decoders yield better accuracy and greater temporal flexibility on real-world tasks. An implementation of INDEQS and the advection simulation is available at https://github.com/Mitchi1/indeqs.

</details>


### [165] [ChronoSurv: A Clinical Pathway-Guided Graph Framework for Multimodal Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19140)
*Hugo Miccinilli,Theo Di Piazza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChronoSurv，一种异构层次化有向图框架，通过感知临床轨迹的图建模实现头颈癌多模态生存分析，在两个公开数据集上取得最佳判别性能与可靠校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型依赖静态融合策略或时间无关建模，无法捕捉结构化临床工作流中的时序依赖关系及多模态间的非对称交互。

Method: 构建临床进展感知的有向图，按关键诊断步骤对齐；设计层次化拓扑（细粒度、粗粒度、全局表示），支持缺失模态的灵活适应；采用异质消息传递机制建模模态间及临床步骤间的复杂非对称关系。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上取得最优判别性能，且校准可靠；消融实验验证了各架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的图建模方法能有效提升多模态生存预测性能，为个性化治疗规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate survival prediction is essential for personalized treatment planning in head and neck cancer, yet remains challenging due to the heterogeneous and high-dimensional nature of multimodal clinical data. While deep survival models have improved predictive performance over classical statistical approaches, existing methods typically rely on static fusion strategies or temporally agnostic modeling, limiting their ability to capture structured clinical workflows. In this work, we propose ChronoSurv, a heterogeneous hierarchical directed graph framework for multimodal survival analysis. ChronoSurv represents patient care as a progression-aware clinical trajectory using directed graphs aligned with key diagnostic steps. A hierarchical topology incorporates fine-grained, coarse, and global representations, further supporting flexible adaptation to missing modalities, while heterogeneous message passing models complex and asymmetric relationships across modalities and clinical steps. Experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that ChronoSurv achieves state-of-the-art discriminative performance while maintaining statistically reliable calibration. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the contribution of each architectural component, highlighting the potential of trajectory-aware graph modeling for multimodal survival prediction.

</details>


### [166] [Complementary Attention Head Pruning for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19150)
*Yaniv Livertovsky,Shahar Somin,Gonen Singer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAHP是一种新颖的后处理剪枝框架，将注意力头选择重新定义为全局图论问题，通过图聚类和信息论距离度量来保留拓扑多样化的互补注意力头，无需预定义稀疏度或手动调参，并在SST-5和MNLI基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法依赖基于梯度的排序或随机门控，存在不稳定性、结构退化以及需要大量手动超参数调优的问题。

Method: CAHP采用图聚类结合信息论距离度量，将头选择建模为全局图论问题；通过识别边际性能递减曲线自动确定每层保留的头数，无需预设剪枝比率。

Result: 在SST-5和MNLI基准上，CAHP在不同Transformer尺度下均优于竞争基线，尤其在高压缩比场景下表现突出。结构分析表明CAHP避免了梯度方法常见的"邻近偏差"，能在中间层保留功能关键的头。

Conclusion: CAHP作为一种无需预定义稀疏度、自动确定剪枝程度的后处理框架，通过保留拓扑多样化的互补注意力头，在高效压缩的同时保持了模型性能。

Abstract: The remarkable success of Transformer-based models in natural language processing stems from architectural scaling, which leads to a large number of parameters and hinders deployment in resource-constrained environments. While structured pruning offers a pathway to compression, existing state-of-the-art methods often rely on gradient-based importance ranking or stochastic gating, which suffer from instability, structural degeneration, and the need for extensive manual hyperparameter tuning. In this paper, we introduce CAHP (Complementary Attention Head Pruning), a novel post-hoc framework that redefines head selection as a global graph-theoretical problem. Rather than evaluating heads in isolation, CAHP utilizes graph-based clustering combined with information-theoretic distance measures to identify and preserve a topologically diverse subset of complementary attention heads. Without requiring a predefined sparsity level or pruning ratio, the framework automatically determines the number of selected attention heads across layers by identifying a diminishing marginal performance curve, where pruning additional heads leads to a sharp degradation in performance, as determined by the chosen polynomial degree. Extensive evaluations on the SST-5 and MNLI benchmarks, across different Transformer model scales, demonstrate that CAHP consistently outperforms competitive baselines, particularly in high-compression regimes. Furthermore, our structural analysis shows that CAHP avoids the "proximity bias" of gradient-based pruning methods, which tend to preserve heads mainly in layers close to the output, and instead retains a functionally critical set of attention heads in the model's intermediate layers.

</details>


### [167] [The Reward Was in Your Data All Along: Correcting Flow Matching with Discriminator-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19162)
*Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Felix Friedrich,Zhang Xiaofeng,Reyhane Askari-Hemmat,Xiaochuang Han,Adriana Romero-Soriano,Michal Drozdzal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对匹配损失（如ℓ2回归）与生成质量之间的结构不匹配问题，提出Discriminator-Guided RL (DRL)，利用预训练表示空间中的判别器logit作为KL正则化RL的奖励，无需人类偏好即可显著提升多种扩散/流匹配模型的生成质量和语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配损失（如ℓ2回归）在训练时边际分布下评估速度/分数场的误差，与推理时决定样本质量的视觉和语义属性不一致，导致模型需要依赖基于偏好的RL来恢复视觉真实性和连贯结构；而人类偏好获取昂贵且混淆了数据真实性与标注者倾向。

Method: 在预训练表示空间中训练判别器区分数据与基模型样本，将其logit作为KL正则化RL的奖励；该logit估计数据与模型间的对数似然比，是瞄准数据分布的最优奖励函数，预训练空间限制判别器关注感知上有意义的方向。

Result: 在SiT、JiT、REPA、RAE等多种骨干网络上，DRL显著降低无引导FID（SiT: 9.38→2.62）和语义空间FD（DINOv3: 88.2→19.3），始终优于基线；同时提升人类偏好奖励而无需在其上训练，并在后续偏好后训练中实现更好的偏好奖励与图像保真度的Pareto前沿，减少过饱和、过亮等低层次伪影。

Conclusion: DRL通过预训练感知空间中的判别器奖励有效弥补了匹配损失的结构性缺陷，无需人类偏好即可同时提升视觉质量和语义对齐，并与后续偏好对齐方法互补。

Abstract: Score- and flow-matching models often rely on preference-based reinforcement learning for two purposes: aligning with subjective preferences and, surprisingly, recovering properties such as visual realism and coherent object structure that matching-based training is intended to learn from the data itself. We argue that this reflects a structural mismatch. Matching losses measure $\ell_2$ regression error on the velocity or score field under training-time marginals, a proxy poorly aligned with the visual and semantic properties that determine sample quality at inference. Given a reward aligned with these properties, RL sidesteps the mismatch by evaluating the model on its own samples and following the reward landscape directly. The challenge is to obtain such a reward without relying on human preferences, which are expensive and conflate data realism with annotator inclinations.
  We propose Discriminator-Guided RL (DRL). DRL trains a discriminator to separate data from base-model samples in a pretrained representation space and uses its logit as the reward in KL-regularized RL. The pretrained space restricts the discriminator to perceptually meaningful directions, and the logit estimates the log-likelihood ratio between data and model, which is the optimal reward for targeting the data distribution. Across SiT, JiT, REPA, and RAE, DRL reduces guidance-free FID (e.g., $9.38 \to 2.62$ on SiT) and semantic-space FD (e.g., $88.2 \to 19.3$ on DINOv3 for SiT), with consistent gains across all backbones, and improves human-preference rewards without training on them. It also yields a better Pareto frontier between preference reward and image fidelity under subsequent preference-based post-training, increasing alignment while reducing low-level artifacts such as oversaturation and excessive brightness.

</details>


### [168] [AGDN: Learning to Solve Traveling Salesman Problem with Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19185)
*Bolin Shen,Ziwei Huang,Zhiguang Cao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出各向异性图扩散网络（AGDN），一种用于求解旅行商问题（TSP）的新型图神经网络框架。该方法通过构建MixScore转移矩阵融合节点相似性与成对距离，并采用各向异性图扩散策略实现多跳信息高效交换，有效解决了全连接TSP图缺乏拓扑先验信息以及图稀疏化后丢失最优解中连通节点两个核心难题。实验表明AGDN在多种实例规模和节点分布下均优于现有方法，且具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习方法在求解TSP时面临两个核心困难：(1) 全连接TSP图缺乏有效的拓扑先验信息，导致GNN难以捕捉图结构特征；(2) 常用的图稀疏化技术会丢失最优解中的关键连通节点，影响求解质量。因此需要一种能更好利用图结构信息的方法。

Method: 提出各向异性图扩散网络（AGDN），核心包括：(1) 构建MixScore转移矩阵，融合节点相似性与成对距离信息；(2) 设计各向异性图扩散策略，支持多跳信息高效交换，弥补全连接图拓扑信息不足；(3) 通过图扩散机制缓解稀疏化导致的关键节点丢失问题。

Result: 在多种实例规模和节点分布下的综合实验表明，AGDN在求解质量上持续优于现有方法，同时计算时间具有竞争力。此外，AGDN对训练时未见过的问题规模和节点分布具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AGDN通过各向异性图扩散有效提升了GNN在TSP上的求解性能，为组合优化问题中的图表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and arises in many practical scenarios. Although graph-based learning approaches have been explored for TSP, the question of how to exploit graph structure more effectively remains open. We present the Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network (AGDN), a new Graph Neural Network framework designed to solve TSP. Our method tackles two central difficulties: (1) the lack of informative topological prior in fully connected TSP graphs, and (2) losing connected nodes in the optimal solution after the commonly used graph sparsification techniques. To overcome these issues, we construct a MixScore transition matrix that merges node similarity with pairwise distance, and we develop an anisotropic graph diffusion strategy that supports efficient information exchange across multiple hops. Comprehensive experiments spanning diverse instance sizes and node distributions show that AGDN consistently outperforms existing methods while keeping computation time competitive. Furthermore, AGDN generalizes well to problem sizes and distributions beyond those seen during training. The implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN.

</details>


### [169] [A Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization Framework for Constraint-Aware Bioprocess Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19230)
*Samuel Stricker,Claus Wirnsperger,Alessandro Butté,Laura Helleckes,Gonzalo Guillén Gosálbez,Antonio del Rio Chanona,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PFGS框架的扩展版本，通过将约束满足概率显式化为帕累托目标以及引入蒙特卡洛采样策略评估输入鲁棒性，使领域专家能够在多目标贝叶斯优化中交互式选择候选点，并在CHO细胞培养模拟中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化方法通常返回单一的自动推荐，难以应对实际工程中同时存在约束条件和输入扰动不确定性的复杂场景，且专家难以介入优化过程以融入领域知识。

Method: 将约束满足的后验概率（从GP后验解析计算）作为显式帕累托目标，同时采用蒙特卡洛采样估计输入扰动下的期望下限性能以表征鲁棒性，通过二维投影交互仪表盘展示多维帕累托前沿。

Result: 在八维CHO细胞培养模拟中，该方法系统性地识别出高性能、满足可行性约束且对扰动鲁棒的操作条件，专家定义的需求提供了原则性的停止准则并支持实验资源的合理分配。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将预测性能、模型不确定性、约束满足概率和输入鲁棒性之间的权衡同时可视化，支持在代理模型改进和开发目标演化过程中迭代优化选择标准。

Abstract: This work presents an extension to Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) Bayesian Optimization (BO) framework in which Gaussian process (GP) surrogate-derived quantities are reformulated as objectives of a multi-objective optimization problem, and the resulting Pareto front is exposed to a domain expert for interactive candidate selection rather than returning a single automated recommendation. The framework is extended in two directions: constrained optimization is addressed by incorporating the posterior probability of satisfying output specification limits as an explicit Pareto objective, computed analytically from the GP posterior distribution; robust optimization is addressed by a Monte Carlo sampling strategy that estimates expected lower-confidence performance over a user-defined variability of input perturbations, capturing performance degradation under likely implementation deviations. The resulting multi-dimensional Pareto representation renders trade-offs between predicted performance, model uncertainty, probabilistic constraint satisfaction, and input robustness simultaneously visible through pairwise two-dimensional projections on an interactive dashboard, enabling selection criteria to be iteratively refined as the surrogate model improves and development objectives evolve. The framework is showcased on an eight-dimensional fed-batch Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cell culture simulator demonstrating systematic identification of high-performing, feasibility-compliant, and perturbation-resilient operating conditions, and illustrating how expert-defined requirements provide a principled stopping criterion and support informed allocation of experimental resources.

</details>


### [170] [SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19255)
*Xingze Zheng,Hanyin Cheng,Siyuan Wang,Yiting Hao,Peng Chen,Yuan Jun,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于多尺度聚类的时序异常检测方法SCAN，通过在表示层引入聚类中心约束和异常标准层结合聚类隶属度概率与重构误差，解决重构方法在异常检测中的过泛化与欠泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的时序异常检测方法存在过泛化（模型也能重构异常）和欠泛化（无法充分重构正常模式）的问题，两者难以平衡。

Method: 1）表示层：引入正常模式的聚类中心表示，约束模型针对代表性正常模式进行重构；2）异常标准层：基于聚类隶属度概率导出异常置信度分数，与重构误差结合形成双标准检测；3）提取邻域中心表示进行多视图聚类以提升聚类性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 多尺度聚类能有效增强基于重构的时序异常检测方法，平衡过泛化与欠泛化问题。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.

</details>


### [171] [Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19262)
*Robi Rahman,Sabiha Tajdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类在对抗环境下的鲁棒性。通过5轮监控-规避迭代，在4代9种GPU模型上评估了20种规避策略，开发的分类器在识别训练工作负载时达到98.2%的二元准确率，对最具挑战性的意外工作负载（即使经过对抗伪装）仍保持43-87%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件启用的GPU工作负载监控是AI计算治理的重要基础，但如果开发者能够轻易击败监控机制，这类方案将不可行。因此需要评估GPU工作负载分类的对抗鲁棒性。

Method: 使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据（与内容无关的信号，仅观测计算的物理效应，不访问模型权重、训练数据或超参数），跨5轮监控-规避迭代，在4代架构的9种GPU模型上评估20种规避策略家族。

Result: 分类器在整个语料库上识别训练工作负载的二元准确率达98.2%，对最具挑战性的意外工作负载（即使经过对抗伪装）仍保持43-87%的准确率。

Conclusion: 基于NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类具有相当程度的对抗鲁棒性，能够在一定程度上抵御开发者的规避攻击，为AI计算治理方案提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.

</details>


### [172] [Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19292)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay,Miguel Contreras,Jessica Sena,Yuanfang Ren,Andrea Davidson,Ziyuan Guan,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Subhash Nerella,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于9个ICU中309名患者的数据，利用四种高效的序列神经网络模型，评估了环境光强和声压水平在不同预测窗口下对谵妄的预测能力。卷积模型表现最佳（AUC=0.80），声音特征是主导预测因子，结合光照可改善短期预测（<1周）。被动环境感知（尤其是声音）为谵妄风险估计提供了临床上有意义的可解释信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU谵妄是常见且严重的并发症，但早期预测和预防仍具挑战。环境因素（环境光强和声压水平）在风险评估中常被忽视，本研究旨在探索这些被动感知的环境变量是否能独立预测谵妄发生。

Method: 在9个ICU中收集309名患者数据，采用四种高效的序列神经网络模型，针对10种不同预测窗口大小进行谵妄预测，并使用Shapley Additive Explanations（SHAP）分析特征重要性与影响方向。

Result: 卷积模型表现最优，在声音数据和组合数据上AUC达0.80；声音特征是整体主导预测因子；结合光照可提升短期（<1周）预测性能，组合模型在感知期后立即赋予最高风险。

Conclusion: 被动环境感知（尤其是声音）能为谵妄风险估计提供临床上有意义的可解释信号，为丰富多模态ICU预测和预防策略提供实用路径。

Abstract: Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.

</details>


### [173] [Does VLA Even Know the Basics? Measuring Commonsense and World Knowledge Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19297)
*Nikita Kachaev,Andrey Moskalenko,Matvey Skripkin,Nikita Kurlaev,Daria Pugacheva,Albina Burlova,Mikhail Kolosov,Denis Shepelev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Elena Tutubalina,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Act2Answer协议，通过将VLM知识基准适应为机器人操作任务（动作即答案），系统评估VLA模型在微调后保留的常识和事实知识。研究发现VLA在简单概念上表现良好，但在丰富语义类别上与源VLM存在更大差距；VQA共训练有助于知识保留；答案相关信号在VLA中间层最强但上层衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常由预训练VLM微调得到，但微调后模型保留了多少常识和事实知识尚不清楚。知识敏感任务上的失败难以区分是知识缺失还是底层控制泛化差，因此需要一种能解耦控制混淆的知识评估方法。

Method: 提出Act2Answer轻量级协议：将VLM知识基准问题改编为桌面操作任务，智能体通过单个物体放置动作来选择候选答案，从而获得动作锚定的成功率以减少控制混淆。同时引入逐层意图探测（layerwise intent probing）定位VLM骨干和动作头中答案相关信息的位置。

Result: 对7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线进行了大规模系统比较，发现：VLA在简单概念上表现扎实，但在丰富语义类别上与源VLM差距更大；VQA共训练与更好的知识保留相关；答案相关信号在VLA中间层达到峰值，但在上层衰减。

Conclusion: Act2Answer提供了一种评估VLA模型知识保留能力的有效方法，揭示了当前VLA模型在知识保留上的不足，并为未来改进提供了方向（如VQA共训练、中间层信息利用）。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Act2Answer is available at https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/.

</details>


### [174] [P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19303)
*Xizhuo,Zhang,Zekai Wang,Fei Liu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理增强的Koopman图卷积网络(P-K-GCN)，用于不规则几何上的时空超分辨率重建，通过样条GCN提取空间依赖、Koopman算子线性化时序动力学、物理损失约束重建结果，并在3D心脏电动力学重建任务上验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真时空动力学模拟计算成本极高，需要高效的超分辨率技术从粗粒度输入重建高分辨率数据。传统数据驱动方法缺乏物理约束，而简单物理信息学习难以处理不规则几何和复杂时序动力学。

Method: 1) 设计连续样条GCN从粗粒度图中提取空间依赖关系；2) 引入Koopman算子理论将非线性动力学投影到紧凑潜空间，实现时间演化线性化；3) 增加基于物理的损失函数增强优化目标，约束重建结果符合物理定律；4) 提供理论分析证明物理增强和Koopman正则化通过降低Rademacher复杂度和收紧泛化界来保证超分辨率误差减小。

Result: 在3D心脏几何上从稀疏低分辨率测量重建高分辨率心脏电动力学，数值实验表明该方法在所有基线模型中取得了最优精度。

Conclusion: P-K-GCN通过融合物理约束与Koopman正则化，在不规则几何的时空超分辨率任务中显著优于传统方法，且理论上保证了泛化误差的降低。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [175] [Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19315)
*Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Pengcheng Wang,Shizhe Diao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diffusion-Proof，首个将扩散语言模型（dLLM）应用于形式化定理证明的框架，包含全局证明生成模型dLLM-Prover-7B和局部校正模型dLLM-Corrector-7B，在ProofNet和MiniF2F基准上分别提升1.61%和6.14%，并成功解决了一个DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B无法解决的IMO问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型在形式化定理证明中存在长程连贯性差和错误累积问题，而扩散语言模型在多token块迭代去噪方面具有优势，但在形式数学领域尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出Diffusion-Proof框架，包含两个模型：1) dLLM-Prover-7B用于全局证明生成，保证长程连贯的策略使用；2) dLLM-Corrector-7B利用扩散模型的填充能力进行局部证明校正，利用双向信息进行大块扩散校正。

Result: 在相同训练集下，相对显著优于自回归基线；ProofNet-Test上绝对提升1.61%，MiniF2F-Test上绝对提升6.14%；成功解决了一个DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B无法解决的IMO问题。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在形式化定理证明中具有独特优势，特别是在长程连贯性和双向信息利用方面，Diffusion-Proof证明了dLLM在这一领域的有效性。

Abstract: Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Sequential Hiring of Contingent Workers Through Learning-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18438)
*Chris Lee,Xiuli Chao,Izak Duenyas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在劳动力生产能力和供给均存在不确定性的临时用工场景中，企业如何在固定团队规模下通过序贯替换决策实现利润最大化，并提出了一个考虑替换成本与招聘延迟的学习型策略DR-UCB，其理论遗憾界与时间维度的下界匹配，且数值实验表明其优于基准策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决临时用工管理中两个关键运营摩擦：工人替换成本高以及招聘存在随机延迟（如先前的合同义务、排期限制或入职流程），使得招聘决策需经过随机延迟才能生效。

Method: 将问题建模为具有昂贵切换成本和延迟动作的随机多臂赌博机，提出DR-UCB策略，通过学习周期序贯做出替换和招聘决策，利用实时生产数据决定何时发起劳动力调整以及替换哪些工人。

Result: DR-UCB策略的领先阶遗憾在时间维度上与其下界匹配，且数值实验表明其优于基准策略。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一种在延迟招聘和替换成本约束下有效的学习型用工管理策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a sequential workforce management problem in a contingent labor setting with uncertainty in both worker production and labor supply. A firm seeks to maximize cumulative profit by maintaining an active team of fixed size while learning worker productivity over time. We emphasize two critical operational frictions in this problem: replacing workers is costly, and workers may not be available immediately for hiring because of, for example, prior job commitments, scheduling constraints, or onboarding procedures. Thus, hiring decisions take effect only after a random delay. We formulate this problem as a stochastic multi-play bandit with costly switching and delayed actions, and develop a learning-based hiring policy, DR-UCB (DelayedReplacement-UCB), that makes replacement and hiring decisions sequentially through learning cycles. In each cycle, the policy uses real-time production data to determine when to initiate workforce changes and which workers to replace and hire. We show that the leading-order regret of the proposed policy matches its lower bound in its dependence on the time horizon. Our numerical experiments show that DR-UCB outperforms benchmark policies.

</details>


### [177] [Intermediate Bilevel Optimization: Modeling Endogenous Follower Tie-Breaking Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18475)
*Maria Bazotte,Margarida Carvalho,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了双水平优化中追随者最优响应的中间性打破机制，提出了一种决策依赖的随机中间双水平优化（I-BO）模型，并通过逆变换和马尔可夫链变换将其转化为外生不确定性下的变换模型（T-I-BO），采用样本平均近似（SAA）求解，实验表明精确建模追随者打破行为对领导者决策至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双水平优化通常假设追随者采取极端乐观或悲观的打破行为，但在实际中追随者的选择往往是决策依赖的随机事件，作者旨在超越这些极端情形，建立更一般的中间性打破模型。

Method: 提出一类内生的决策依赖概率度量（包括强弱决策依赖的I-BO特例）；通过逆变换和马尔可夫链变换将I-BO转化为等价的外生不确定性T-I-BO模型；采用样本平均近似（SAA）处理不确定性，并针对不同变换设计定制化求解方法。

Result: 所提方法能高效求解合理规模的实例，性能优于确定等价方法；实验揭示错误指定追随者打破行为（特别是与领导者目标对齐程度）会导致领导者决策显著次优。

Conclusion: 准确建模追随者的中间性打破行为对于双水平优化中的领导者决策至关重要，本文提出的I-BO框架为这一方向提供了理论基础和有效算法。

Abstract: In bilevel optimization, optimistic and pessimistic follower behaviors are the most commonly used forms to define how the follower ties-breaks among multiple optimal solutions. In this work, we go beyond these extreme tie-breaking behaviors and investigate the intermediate bilevel optimization program (I-BO), where the follower's selected optimal response is a decision-dependent random event, with a probability measure influenced by the leader's decision. We formally introduce a class of such endogenous measures, including the special case of strong-weak decision-dependent I-BO. We reformulate the I-BO as a Transformed I-BO (T-I-BO) with exogenous uncertainty by defining inverse and Markov-chain transformations, which represent the follower's response as a function of the leader's decision and exogenous randomness. We handle the T-I-BO's uncertainty via sample-average approximation (SAA), and we propose tailored approaches for its SAA program according to the chosen transformation. Computationally, our methods solve reasonable-sized instances efficiently and outperform the deterministic equivalent when available. Furthermore, experiments stress the critical need to accurately model follower tie-breaking behavior, particularly depending on its alignment with the leader's objective, as misspecification leads to suboptimal leader decisions.

</details>


### [178] [Fast primal-dual methods for convex-concave bilinear saddle point problems: continuous-time dynamics and discrete algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18724)
*Xin He,Ya-Ping Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究凸-凹双线性鞍点问题的Nesterov加速方法，针对带消失阻尼α/t（α≥3）的二阶原始-对偶动力系统，在纯凸-凹设定下证明了轨迹收敛到鞍点，并在非临界情形（α>3）获得改进的o(1/t²)原始-对偶间隙率和o(1/t)速度率；通过保结构有限差分离散得到带Nesterov外推的快速原始-对偶算法，对一般加速参数序列{t_k}证明了O(1/t_k²)收敛率，非临界情形（ρ<1）进一步获得o(1/t_k²)间隙率和o(1/t_k)平稳性残差率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为凸-凹双线性鞍点问题在纯凸-凹设定下设计连续和离散的Nesterov加速方法，并获得最优收敛速率。

Method: 建立带消失阻尼α/t的二阶原始-对偶动力系统作为连续模型，通过Lyapunov分析证明收敛性和速率；采用保结构有限差分离散化，得到带Nesterov外推的快速原始-对偶算法，基于参数序列条件t_{k+1}²-t_k²≤ρt_{k+1}进行分析。

Result: 连续模型：α≥3时轨迹收敛到鞍点；α>3时原始-对偶间隙o(1/t²)、速度o(1/t)，额外Lipschitz梯度假设下平稳性残差o(1/t)。离散算法：对ρ∈(0,1]有O(1/t_k²)间隙率；ρ<1时进一步得到o(1/t_k²)间隙率和o(1/t_k)平稳性残差率。

Conclusion: 为纯凸-凹双线性鞍点问题提供了连续和离散层面的统一加速框架，所提算法在非临界情形下达到最优加速收敛速率。

Abstract: This paper studies Nesterov accelerated methods for continuously differentiable convex-concave bilinear saddle point problems. For the continuous-time model, we analyze a second-order primal-dual dynamical system with vanishing damping $α/t$, where $α\geq 3$. Under the merely convex-concave setting, we prove convergence of the primal-dual trajectory to a saddle point. In the noncritical regime $α>3$, we further obtain the improved rate $o(1/t^{2})$ for the primal-dual gap and $o(1/t)$ for the velocity, and, under an additional Lipschitz gradient assumption, $o(1/t)$ for the stationarity residual. We then derive a structure-preserving finite-difference discretization, which leads to a fast primal-dual algorithm with Nesterov extrapolation. For a general accelerated parameter sequence ${t_k}$ satisfying $t_{k+1}^2-t_k^2\le ρt_{k+1}$ with $ρ\in(0,1]$, we prove the $O(1/t_k^{2})$ convergence rate for the primal-dual gap and convergence of the generated sequence. In the noncritical case $ρ<1$, we further establish the improved rate $o(1/t_k^{2})$ for the gap and $o(1/t_k)$ for the stationarity residual. These results provide continuous-discrete acceleration methods for bilinear saddle point problems in the merely convex-concave setting.

</details>


### [179] [Sub-optimal control by primal-dual gradient dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18818)
*Arjan van der Schaft*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将连续时间原始-对偶梯度算法的端口-哈密顿形式从静态约束凸优化推广到凸最优控制问题，得到的动力学被证明是一个包含物理时间和“算法”时间的偏微分方程端口-哈密顿系统，并展示了收敛到最优控制解的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将端口-哈密顿框架从静态凸优化扩展到凸最优控制问题，以统一的方式描述优化算法与物理系统的耦合。

Method: 通过推广连续时间原始-对偶梯度算法的端口-哈密顿形式，将静态优化问题扩展到凸最优控制问题，构建偏微分方程形式的端口-哈密顿系统。

Result: 所得到的动力学是一个包含物理时间和算法时间的偏微分方程端口-哈密顿系统，能够收敛到最优控制解。

Conclusion: 该偏微分方程形式可以用于推导次优控制策略，为优化算法的物理实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This note generalizes the port-Hamiltonian formulation of the continuous time primal-dual gradient algorithm for static constrained convex optimization to the convex optimal control problem.The resulting dynamics is shown to be a port-Hamiltonian system of partial differential equations, involving ordinary physical time as well 'algorithmic' time. Convergence to the optimal control solution is indicated, and it is argued that sub-optimal control strategies could be derived starting from the partial differential equation formulation.

</details>


### [180] [Analyzing changes in optimal variables in linear programming with uncertain parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18830)
*Baptiste Istace,Guillaume Derval,Bardhyl Miftari,Quentin Louveaux*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性问题中参数不确定性对最优变量值的影响，分析了三种线性修改（成本向量、右侧项、约束矩阵）的敏感性，并建立了连续性、局部凸性和凹性等性质定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性问题常包含许多不确定参数，传统敏感性分析聚焦于目标函数，而本文转向分析这些参数如何影响变量的最优值。

Method: 考虑三类线性修改：成本向量修改、右侧项修改和约束矩阵修改，通过理论推导建立最优变量值的相关性质。

Result: 建立了最优变量值在三种修改下的连续性条件，以及局部凸性和凹性等性质定理。

Conclusion: 将敏感性分析从目标函数扩展到最优变量值，为线性规划中的参数不确定性提供了更全面的分析框架。

Abstract: Linear problems often include many parameters that may be uncertain. Sensitivity analysis studies how these parameters impact optimal values. Instead of analyzing the objective function, we shift the focus to the optimal values of the variables. Three types of linear modifications are considered: on the cost vector, the right-hand side, and on the constraint matrix. Several theorems establish properties of these modifications, including conditions for continuity of optimal variable values, as well as local convexity and concavity properties.

</details>


### [181] [Optimal Consumption and Retirement Time under Shortfall Risk Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18935)
*Lijun Bo,Yijie Huang,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在基准跟踪框架下研究最优投资组合、消费和内生的提前退休问题，通过引入新的相对业绩评估机制。投资者在最大化终身消费效用的同时，管理相对于基准的最大财富缺口，且退休前后的缺口管理成本可能不同。通过凸对偶定理将原问题转化为带状态反射的二维纯最优停时问题，并推导出最优退休边界及投资消费策略。分析显示退休后投资更保守、消费更激进，预期最大缺口风险呈U型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对基准跟踪框架下的退休决策问题关注不足，特别是退休前后缺口管理成本不对称的情形。本文旨在建立包含相对业绩评估、最优停时（退休决策）和混合随机控制的统一分析框架，以刻画投资者在基准跟踪约束下的退休时机、投资和消费行为。
